const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const esc = s => String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
const KEY = 'swsh-library-v1';
const store = { get() { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; } }, set(p) { try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(Object.assign(store.get(), p))); } catch (e) {} } };
const reduceMotion = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
const todayStr = () => { const d = new Date(); return `${d.getFullYear()}-${d.getMonth() + 1}-${d.getDate()}`; };
function toast(msg, ms = 2600) { const t = document.createElement('div'); t.className = 'toast'; t.textContent = msg; document.body.appendChild(t); setTimeout(() => t.remove(), ms); }
const opacUrl = q => CONFIG.opacSearch && q ? CONFIG.opacSearch.replace('{q}', encodeURIComponent(q)) : CONFIG.opacHome;
function opacGo(q) {
  q = (q || '').trim();
  if (q && !CONFIG.opacSearch) { try { navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText(q); } catch (e) {} toast(`已複製「${q}」，在 WebPAC 貼上就能查詢囉！`, 3600); }
  window.open(opacUrl(q), '_blank', 'noopener');
}

// =====================================================================
//  音效（只有一個開關）
// =====================================================================
const SFX = (() => {
  let ac = null, on = store.get().sound !== false;
  const ctx = () => { if (!ac) { try { ac = new (window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext)(); } catch (e) { return null; } } if (ac.state === 'suspended') ac.resume(); return ac; };
  const tone = (f, d, type = 'sine', v = 0.12, slide = 0, delay = 0) => { if (!on) return; const a = ctx(); if (!a) return; const t = a.currentTime + delay, o = a.createOscillator(), g = a.createGain(); o.type = type; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f, t); if (slide) o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(40, f + slide), t + d); g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(v, t + 0.012); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + d); o.connect(g).connect(a.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t + d + 0.05); };
  const noise = (d, v = 0.08, f = 1200, delay = 0, q = 1.2) => { if (!on) return; const a = ctx(); if (!a) return; const t = a.currentTime + delay, n = Math.floor(a.sampleRate * d), b = a.createBuffer(1, n, a.sampleRate), ch = b.getChannelData(0); for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) ch[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / n); const s = a.createBufferSource(); s.buffer = b; const fl = a.createBiquadFilter(); fl.type = 'bandpass'; fl.frequency.value = f; fl.Q.value = q; const g = a.createGain(); g.gain.value = v; s.connect(fl).connect(g).connect(a.destination); s.start(t); };
  const bark = (delay) => { if (!on) return; const a = ctx(); if (!a) return; const t = a.currentTime + delay, o = a.createOscillator(), f = a.createBiquadFilter(), g = a.createGain(); o.type = 'sawtooth'; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(520, t); o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(260, t + 0.13); f.type = 'lowpass'; f.frequency.setValueAtTime(1800, t); f.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(500, t + 0.14); g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.22, t + 0.015); g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t + 0.16); o.connect(f).connect(g).connect(a.destination); o.start(t); o.stop(t + 0.2); noise(0.08, 0.05, 900, delay); };
  return {
    get on() { return on; }, toggle() { on = !on; store.set({ sound: on }); if (on) this.pop(); return on; },
    pop() { tone(480, 0.12, 'sine', 0.12, 420); }, chime() { [784, 988, 1319].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, 0.4, 'triangle', 0.06, 0, i * 0.07)); },
    woof() { bark(0); bark(0.2); }, giggle() { tone(720, 0.09, 'sine', 0.09, 260); tone(820, 0.09, 'sine', 0.09, 260, 0.12); tone(940, 0.12, 'sine', 0.08, 300, 0.24); },
    hmm() { tone(330, 0.18, 'triangle', 0.09, 90); tone(440, 0.22, 'triangle', 0.08, 120, 0.2); }, tap() { tone(1200, 0.05, 'square', 0.03); tone(1600, 0.06, 'square', 0.025, 0, 0.07); },
    crank() { for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { noise(0.035, 0.09, 2600, i * 0.1, 3); tone(900 + (i % 2) * 120, 0.03, 'square', 0.02, 0, i * 0.1); } },
    drop() { tone(420, 0.12, 'sine', 0.14, -200); tone(330, 0.1, 'sine', 0.1, -150, 0.2); tone(260, 0.08, 'sine', 0.07, -100, 0.33); },
    sparkle() { [1047, 1319, 1568, 2093].forEach((f, i) => tone(f, 0.3, 'sine', 0.06, 0, i * 0.07)); },
    radio() { noise(0.3, 0.04, 3200, 0, 0.6); tone(523, 0.18, 'triangle', 0.07, 0, 0.32); tone(659, 0.18, 'triangle', 0.07, 0, 0.46); tone(784, 0.3, 'triangle', 0.07, 0, 0.6); },
    whoosh() { noise(0.5, 0.06, 700, 0, 0.5); tone(300, 0.5, 'sine', 0.05, 600); }
  };
})();

// =====================================================================
//  3D 場景
// =====================================================================
let renderer = null;
try { renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true }); } catch (e) { renderer = null; }
const scene = new THREE.Scene();
const portrait = () => innerWidth < innerHeight * 0.9;
const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(32, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.5, 2000);
const cam = { yaw: 0.28, pitch: 0.3, dist: 64, tYaw: 0.28, tPitch: 0.3, tDist: 64 };
const camTarget = new THREE.Vector3(0, 5.2, 0);
const tex = (w, h, draw) => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = w; c.height = h; draw(c.getContext('2d'), w, h); const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); if (renderer) t.anisotropy = Math.min(8, renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy()); t.encoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding; return t; };
// 黏土質感：細微的手捏凹凸
const CLAY = (() => { const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = c.height = 128; const x = c.getContext('2d'); x.fillStyle = '#808080'; x.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 128); for (let i = 0; i < 900; i++) { const v = 100 + Math.random() * 60 | 0; x.fillStyle = `rgba(${v},${v},${v},.35)`; x.beginPath(); x.arc(Math.random() * 128, Math.random() * 128, 1 + Math.random() * 3, 0, 7); x.fill(); } const t = new THREE.CanvasTexture(c); t.wrapS = t.wrapT = THREE.RepeatWrapping; return t; })();
const matCache = {};
const MAT = (color, o) => { if (o) return new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial(Object.assign({ color, roughness: 0.82, metalness: 0, bumpMap: CLAY, bumpScale: 0.012 }, o)); const k = String(color); return matCache[k] || (matCache[k] = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color, roughness: 0.82, metalness: 0, bumpMap: CLAY, bumpScale: 0.012 })); };
const _lin = new WeakSet();
function linearize(root) { root.traverse(o => { if (!o.material) return; (Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : [o.material]).forEach(m => { if (_lin.has(m)) return; _lin.add(m); if (m.color && !m.vertexColors) m.color.convertSRGBToLinear(); if (m.emissive) m.emissive.convertSRGBToLinear(); }); }); }
const mesh = (g, m, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0, cast = true) => { const o = new THREE.Mesh(g, m); o.position.set(x, y, z); o.castShadow = cast; o.receiveShadow = true; return o; };
const geoCache = {};
function rbox(w, h, d, r = 0.3) {
  const k = [w, h, d, r].join('|'); if (geoCache[k]) return geoCache[k];
  r = Math.max(0.01, Math.min(r, w / 2 - 0.01, h / 2 - 0.01, d / 2 - 0.01));
  const s = new THREE.Shape(), x0 = -w / 2 + r, y0 = -h / 2 + r, iw = w - 2 * r, ih = h - 2 * r;
  s.moveTo(x0, y0); s.lineTo(x0 + iw, y0); s.lineTo(x0 + iw, y0 + ih); s.lineTo(x0, y0 + ih); s.lineTo(x0, y0);
  const g = new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(s, { depth: Math.max(0.001, d - 2 * r), bevelEnabled: true, bevelThickness: r, bevelSize: r, bevelSegments: 4, curveSegments: 4 });
  g.translate(0, 0, -Math.max(0.001, d - 2 * r) / 2); g.computeVertexNormals(); return (geoCache[k] = g);
}
function rr(x, X, Y, W, H, R) { x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(X + R, Y); x.arcTo(X + W, Y, X + W, Y + H, R); x.arcTo(X + W, Y + H, X, Y + H, R); x.arcTo(X, Y + H, X, Y, R); x.arcTo(X, Y, X + W, Y, R); x.closePath(); }
const SPH = new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 28, 20);
const Bx = (g, w, h, d, c, x, y, z, r = 0.15) => { const m = mesh(rbox(w, h, d, r), MAT(c), x, y, z); g.add(m); return m; };
const Cy = (g, rt, rb, h, c, x, y, z, seg = 24) => { const m = mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(rt, rb, h, seg), MAT(c), x, y, z); g.add(m); return m; };
const Sp = (g, r, c, x, y, z, sx = 1, sy = 1, sz = 1) => { const m = mesh(SPH, MAT(c), x, y, z); m.scale.set(r * sx, r * sy, r * sz); g.add(m); return m; };
const To = (g, R, r, c, x, y, z, arc = Math.PI * 2) => { const m = mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(R, r, 12, 32, arc), MAT(c), x, y, z); g.add(m); return m; };
const Co = (g, r, h, c, x, y, z, seg = 24) => { const m = mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(r, h, seg), MAT(c), x, y, z); g.add(m); return m; };
const Pl = (g, w, h, map, x, y, z, o = {}) => { const m = mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w, h), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial(Object.assign({ map, roughness: 0.75 }, o)), x, y, z, false); g.add(m); return m; };
const G = (par, x = 0, y = 0, z = 0) => { const g = new THREE.Group(); g.position.set(x, y, z); par.add(g); return g; };
const FONT = '"Noto Sans TC","PingFang TC","Microsoft JhengHei",sans-serif', RF = '"Huninn","Noto Sans TC",sans-serif';

const ANIM = [];          // 每格更新的小動畫
const HOTS = {};          // 可點擊熱點
const pickables = [];
const CHARS = {};
const BOOKS = [];         // 書架上的書（最後合併成 InstancedMesh）

function hot(id, g, ay, label, icon, col) {
  g.userData.hot = id; HOTS[id] = { g, ay, label, icon, col };
  g.traverse(o => { if (o.isMesh) pickables.push(o); });
}

// ---------- 角色：四維 IP（黏土小人） ----------
function chibi(o) {
  const SK = '#FFE1CC', HAIR = '#3B2B25', SHIRT = '#FFFFFF', PANTS = '#2F2F3C';
  const g = new THREE.Group(), body = G(g);
  const s = o.tall || 1;
  const legs = [-1, 1].map(k => { const p = G(body, k * 0.27, 1.0 * s, 0); Cy(p, 0.21, 0.19, 0.85 * s, PANTS, 0, -0.42 * s, 0, 14); Sp(p, 0.25, '#FFFFFF', 0, -0.9 * s, 0.07, 1, 0.6, 1.35); return p; });
  Cy(body, 0.5, 0.62, 1.15, SHIRT, 0, 1.5 * s + 0.05, 0, 24);
  Cy(body, 0.63, 0.63, 0.16, PANTS, 0, 1.0 * s + 0.03, 0, 24);
  [-1, 1].forEach(k => { const c = mesh(rbox(0.42, 0.1, 0.3, 0.04), MAT('#FFFFFF'), k * 0.2, 2.06 * s, 0.4); c.rotation.z = -k * 0.45; c.rotation.y = k * 0.3; body.add(c); });
  [0, 1, 2].forEach(k => Sp(body, 0.045, '#C9C2BC', 0, 1.85 * s - k * 0.26, 0.56));
  Bx(body, 0.2, 0.22, 0.05, '#FFFFFF', 0.25, 1.68 * s, 0.53, 0.04); Sp(body, 0.06, '#E8444B', 0.25, 1.7 * s, 0.57, 1, 1, 0.5);
  const arms = [-1, 1].map(k => { const p = G(body, k * 0.6, 1.92 * s, 0); p.rotation.z = k * 0.16; Cy(p, 0.17, 0.15, 0.55, SHIRT, 0, -0.22, 0, 12); Sp(p, 0.15, SK, 0, -0.6, 0); return p; });
  const head = G(body, 0, 2.95 * s, 0);
  Sp(head, 0.98, SK, 0, 0, 0, 1.06, 0.98, 1);
  // 眼睛
  if (o.sleepy) [-1, 1].forEach(k => { const e = Bx(head, 0.24, 0.06, 0.05, '#2B2230', k * 0.36, -0.02, 0.96, 0.025); e.rotation.z = k * 0.08; });
  else [-1, 1].forEach(k => { Sp(head, 0.1, '#2B2230', k * 0.36, -0.02, 0.95, 0.9, 1.15, 0.5); Sp(head, 0.032, '#FFFFFF', k * 0.36 + 0.035, 0.04, 1.0); });
  if (o.blush !== false) [-1, 1].forEach(k => Sp(head, 0.14, '#FFA9B8', k * 0.6, -0.26, 0.78, 1.3, 0.75, 0.4));
  const m = mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(0.07, 0.022, 8, 16, Math.PI), MAT('#7A3B3B'), 0, -0.3, 0.97); m.rotation.z = Math.PI; head.add(m);
  // 頭髮
  const cap = mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1, 28, 16, 0, Math.PI * 2, 0, Math.PI * 0.52), MAT(HAIR), 0, 0.12, -0.04); cap.scale.set(1.13, 1.05, 1.08); head.add(cap);
  Sp(head, 1, HAIR, 0, -0.08, -0.3, 1.1, 0.98, 0.85);
  if (o.hair === 'curly') { for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) { const a = i * 2.4, r = 0.5 + (i % 4) * 0.16, y = 0.5 + Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, 1 - r * r)) * 0.5; Sp(head, 0.34, HAIR, Math.cos(a) * r * 1.1, y + 0.1, Math.sin(a) * r * 0.95 - 0.08); } [-1, 1].forEach(k => Sp(head, 0.36, HAIR, k * 0.95, 0.1, 0.1)); Sp(head, 0.3, HAIR, 0.2, 0.55, 0.78, 1.2, 0.8, 0.7); Sp(head, 0.28, HAIR, -0.35, 0.58, 0.72, 1.2, 0.8, 0.7); }
  else { const bang = Sp(head, 1, HAIR, 0, 0.5, 0.58, 0.92, 0.34, 0.48); if (o.hair === 'boy') { bang.scale.set(0.95, 0.3, 0.5); bang.position.y = 0.55; } }
  if (o.hair === 'bob') [-1, 1].forEach(k => Sp(head, 1, HAIR, k * 0.86, -0.28, 0.05, 0.3, 0.72, 0.62));
  if (o.hair === 'boy') [-1, 1].forEach(k => Sp(head, 1, HAIR, k * 0.92, 0.05, -0.05, 0.22, 0.5, 0.5));
  if (o.hair === 'long') { [-1, 1].forEach(k => Sp(head, 1, HAIR, k * 0.86, -0.6, 0.0, 0.3, 1.05, 0.55)); Sp(head, 1, HAIR, 0, -0.95, -0.42, 1.0, 1.15, 0.5); }
  if (o.glasses) { [-1, 1].forEach(k => { To(head, 0.21, 0.035, '#E9A0B8', k * 0.36, -0.02, 1.0); }); Bx(head, 0.2, 0.04, 0.04, '#E9A0B8', 0, 0.02, 1.02, 0.02); }
  if (o.clip === 'heart') { const h = G(head, -0.55, 0.52, 0.62); h.rotation.z = 0.3; Sp(h, 0.13, '#E8444B', -0.08, 0.05, 0); Sp(h, 0.13, '#E8444B', 0.08, 0.05, 0); const c = Co(h, 0.17, 0.22, '#E8444B', 0, -0.1, 0, 16); c.rotation.z = Math.PI; }
  if (o.clip === 'pink') { const c = Bx(head, 0.32, 0.1, 0.08, '#FF9EC4', 0.52, 0.5, 0.66, 0.04); c.rotation.z = -0.5; }
  return { g, body, head, arms, legs, ph: Math.random() * 6, jump: -9 };
}

function dachshund() {
  const C = '#EAC28E', E = '#B9773F', g = new THREE.Group(), body = G(g);
  Sp(body, 1, C, 0, 0.5, 0, 0.55, 0.45, 1.45);
  [[-0.3, 0.9], [0.3, 0.9], [-0.3, -0.9], [0.3, -0.9]].forEach(([x, z]) => Sp(body, 0.2, C, x * 1.1, 0.15, z + 0.15, 1, 0.6, 1.4));
  const head = G(body, 0, 0.62, 1.35);
  Sp(head, 0.5, C, 0, 0.1, 0, 1, 0.92, 1);
  Sp(head, 0.32, C, 0, -0.04, 0.48, 0.85, 0.72, 1.25);
  Sp(head, 0.09, '#3A2A2A', 0, 0.04, 0.88);
  [-1, 1].forEach(k => { Sp(head, 0.075, '#2B2230', k * 0.22, 0.2, 0.42); const ear = Sp(head, 0.3, E, k * 0.46, -0.12, -0.05, 0.38, 1.0, 0.7); ear.rotation.z = k * 0.15; });
  const hp = G(head, 0, 0.5, 0.15); hp.rotation.x = -0.4; Sp(hp, 0.09, E, -0.07, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0.5); Sp(hp, 0.09, E, 0.07, 0, 0, 1, 1, 0.5); const hc = Co(hp, 0.12, 0.14, E, 0, -0.08, 0, 12); hc.rotation.z = Math.PI; hc.scale.z = 0.5;
  const tail = G(body, 0, 0.6, -1.35); const tm = Cy(tail, 0.06, 0.12, 0.8, C, 0, 0.3, -0.2, 10); tm.rotation.x = -0.8;
  // 項圈＋名牌
  const col = To(body, 0.36, 0.06, '#FF8FB8', 0, 0.62, 1.0); col.scale.set(1, 1, 1);
  Sp(body, 0.1, '#FFD54F', 0, 0.3, 1.18);
  return { g, body, head, tail, ph: 0, jump: -9 };
}

// ---------- 小物件 ----------
function bookcase(par, x, y, z, w, h, rows, ry = 0, wood = '#E8C9A0') {
  const g = G(par, x, y, z); g.rotation.y = ry;
  Bx(g, w, h, 0.22, '#C9A57C', 0, h / 2, -0.45, 0.06);
  [-1, 1].forEach(k => Bx(g, 0.26, h, 1.1, wood, k * (w / 2 - 0.13), h / 2, 0, 0.1));
  Bx(g, w, 0.3, 1.15, wood, 0, h - 0.15, 0, 0.12); Bx(g, w, 0.24, 1.1, wood, 0, 0.12, 0, 0.08);
  const sh = (h - 0.4) / rows;
  for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {
    const y0 = 0.2 + r * sh; Bx(g, w - 0.2, 0.12, 1.0, wood, 0, y0, 0.02, 0.04);
    let bx = -w / 2 + 0.32;
    while (bx < w / 2 - 0.4) { const bw = 0.16 + Math.random() * 0.14, bh = sh * (0.62 + Math.random() * 0.25); if (Math.random() < 0.08) { bx += 0.3; continue; } BOOKS.push({ par: g, x: bx + bw / 2, y: y0 + 0.06 + bh / 2, z: 0.05, w: bw, h: bh, d: 0.7, rz: Math.random() < 0.1 ? 0.22 : 0 }); bx += bw + 0.02; }
  }
  return g;
}
function plant(par, x, z, s = 1, y = 0, pot = '#FFB48F') {
  const g = G(par, x, y, z); g.scale.setScalar(s);
  Cy(g, 0.55, 0.42, 0.8, pot, 0, 0.4, 0, 16); To(g, 0.52, 0.08, pot, 0, 0.8, 0).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2;
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const a = i / 7 * Math.PI * 2, l = Sp(g, 0.42, i % 2 ? '#6CC77A' : '#8EDB8A', Math.cos(a) * 0.32, 1.25 + (i % 3) * 0.2, Math.sin(a) * 0.32, 0.55, 1.2, 0.35); l.rotation.set(Math.sin(a) * 0.5, -a, Math.cos(a) * 0.5); }
  return g;
}
function tree(par, x, z, s = 1, col = '#8EDB8A', fruit) {
  const g = G(par, x, 0, z); g.scale.setScalar(s);
  Cy(g, 0.3, 0.42, 2.0, '#C9976A', 0, 1.0, 0, 12);
  [[0, 2.9, 0, 1.5], [0.9, 2.5, 0.3, 1.0], [-0.85, 2.6, -0.2, 1.05], [0.1, 3.7, 0, 1.0]].forEach(([a, b, d, r]) => Sp(g, r, col, a, b, d));
  if (fruit) for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) { const a = k / 6 * Math.PI * 2; Sp(g, 0.2, fruit, Math.cos(a) * 1.35, 2.6 + (k % 2) * 0.7, Math.sin(a) * 1.2); }
  ANIM.push(t => { g.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 0.8 + x) * 0.015; });
  return g;
}
function cloud(par, x, y, z, s = 1) {
  const g = G(par, x, y, z); g.scale.setScalar(s);
  [[0, 0, 0, 1.6], [1.5, -0.2, 0.2, 1.15], [-1.5, -0.25, 0.1, 1.2], [0.6, 0.7, -0.2, 1.1], [-0.7, 0.6, 0.1, 1.0]].forEach(([a, b, c, r]) => { const m = mesh(SPH, MAT('#FFFFFF', { roughness: 1, emissive: 0xffffff, emissiveIntensity: 0.12 }), a, b, c, false); m.scale.set(r, r * 0.82, r); g.add(m); });
  return g;
}
function textTex(w, h, draw) { return tex(w, h, draw); }

// ---------- 建立整個場景 ----------
function buildScene() {
  renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2)); renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
  renderer.outputEncoding = THREE.sRGBEncoding; renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping; renderer.toneMappingExposure = 0.92;
  renderer.shadowMap.enabled = true; renderer.shadowMap.type = THREE.PCFSoftShadowMap;
  $('#stage').appendChild(renderer.domElement);

  // 天空
  const skyG = new THREE.SphereGeometry(900, 32, 16), cols = [], top = new THREE.Color('#4DB3EE'), mid = new THREE.Color('#A8DEFA'), bot = new THREE.Color('#FFF1E0');
  for (let i = 0; i < skyG.attributes.position.count; i++) { const y = skyG.attributes.position.getY(i) / 900, c = y > 0 ? mid.clone().lerp(top, Math.pow(y, 0.6)) : mid.clone().lerp(bot, Math.min(1, -y * 4)); c.convertSRGBToLinear(); cols.push(c.r, c.g, c.b); }
  skyG.setAttribute('color', new THREE.Float32BufferAttribute(cols, 3));
  scene.add(new THREE.Mesh(skyG, new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ vertexColors: true, side: THREE.BackSide, fog: false, depthWrite: false, toneMapped: false })));
  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(0xCDEBFF, 220, 900);
  scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xF4FBFF, 0xB89878, 0.42));
  const sun = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xFFF2DC, 1.15); sun.position.set(30, 60, 45); sun.castShadow = true; sun.shadow.mapSize.set(2048, 2048);
  Object.assign(sun.shadow.camera, { left: -40, right: 40, top: 40, bottom: -40, near: 5, far: 160 }); sun.shadow.bias = -0.0005; sun.shadow.normalBias = 0.03; sun.shadow.radius = 5; scene.add(sun);
  const fill = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xDDE8FF, 0.38); fill.position.set(-40, 25, 30); scene.add(fill);
  const warm1 = new THREE.PointLight(0xFFD6A0, 0.6, 30, 2); warm1.position.set(0, 4.9, -0.4); scene.add(warm1);
  const warm2 = new THREE.PointLight(0xFFE3B8, 0.45, 28, 2); warm2.position.set(0, 11.2, -4.2); scene.add(warm2);

  // 遠景：花蓮的海與中央山脈
  const sea = mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(700, 48), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#7FD0E6', roughness: 0.4 }), 0, -26, 0, false); sea.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(sea);
  for (let i = 0; i < 16; i++) { const a = Math.PI + 0.25 + i / 15 * (Math.PI - 0.5), r = 480 + (i % 3) * 40, h = 38 + (i * 37 % 30); const m = mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(60 + (i % 4) * 12, h, 7), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: ['#6FBFA8', '#86CDB6', '#5DB39C', '#9AD6C2'][i % 4], roughness: 1, flatShading: true }), Math.cos(a) * r, -26 + h / 2, Math.sin(a) * r, false); m.rotation.y = i; scene.add(m); }
  const clouds = [];
  [[-70, 34, -90, 4], [55, 42, -110, 5], [-30, 50, -150, 6], [90, 26, -40, 3], [-95, 22, -20, 3.4], [20, 36, -80, 3]].forEach(([x, y, z, s]) => clouds.push(cloud(scene, x, y, z, s)));
  ANIM.push(t => clouds.forEach((c, k) => { c.position.x += 0.015 + k * 0.003; if (c.position.x > 140) c.position.x = -140; }));

  // 漂浮小島
  const isle = G(scene);
  const top0 = mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(31, 31, 1.6, 64), MAT('#9AD98C'), 0, -0.8, 0); top0.scale.z = 0.78; isle.add(top0);
  const soil = mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(31.2, 28.5, 3.2, 64), MAT('#F2CFA0'), 0, -3.0, 0); soil.scale.z = 0.78; isle.add(soil);
  const rock = mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(27.5, 18, 9), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#D9B48A', roughness: 1, flatShading: true }), 0, -12.6, 0); rock.rotation.x = Math.PI; rock.scale.z = 0.78; isle.add(rock);
  for (let i = 0; i < 28; i++) { const a = i / 28 * Math.PI * 2; Sp(isle, 0.5 + (i % 3) * 0.25, ['#FFD6E5', '#FFFFFF', '#FFE07A', '#D7F5EA'][i % 4], Math.cos(a) * 30.6, -0.05, Math.sin(a) * 30.6 * 0.78, 1, 0.6, 1); }
  ANIM.push(t => { isle.position.y = Math.sin(t * 0.6) * 0.12; });
  const W = G(isle); // 地面上的所有東西

  // 小路
  for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) { const s = mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.95 + (i % 2) * 0.2, 1.0, 0.16, 12), MAT(i % 2 ? '#FFF3DD' : '#F7E6C8'), (i % 2 - 0.5) * 0.7, 0.05, 9.4 + i * 1.75); W.add(s); }

  // ===== 圖書館主體（剖面娃娃屋） =====
  const L = G(W, 0, 0, -1.6); L.scale.setScalar(1.3);
  const WALL = '#FFF4E4', WALL2 = '#FFE4EC', FLOOR1 = '#F6D7A8', FLOOR2 = '#E9D6F7';
  Bx(L, 21, 0.5, 13, '#E7C08F', 0, 0.15, 0, 0.2);
  const fl1 = tex(512, 320, (c, w, h) => { c.fillStyle = FLOOR1; c.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); c.strokeStyle = 'rgba(160,110,60,.25)'; c.lineWidth = 3; for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 32) { c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(0, y); c.lineTo(w, y); c.stroke(); for (let x = (y / 32 % 2) * 64; x < w; x += 128) { c.beginPath(); c.moveTo(x, y); c.lineTo(x, y + 32); c.stroke(); } } });
  const f1 = Pl(L, 20.2, 12.2, fl1, 0, 0.42, 0); f1.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; f1.receiveShadow = true;
  // 牆：後、左、右（前面打開）
  Bx(L, 20.6, 10.2, 0.6, WALL, 0, 5.3, -6.2, 0.2);
  [-1, 1].forEach(k => { Bx(L, 0.6, 10.2, 12.6, WALL, k * 10.0, 5.3, 0, 0.2);
    for (let f = 0; f < 2; f++) for (let j = 0; j < 2; j++) { const wz = -3 + j * 5, wy = 2.8 + f * 5; Bx(L, 0.7, 2.4, 2.0, '#FFFFFF', k * 10.0, wy, wz, 0.25); const gl = mesh(rbox(0.3, 1.9, 1.5, 0.12), MAT('#BEE6FF', { emissive: 0xBEE6FF, emissiveIntensity: 0.25, roughness: 0.3 }), k * 10.05, wy, wz); L.add(gl); Bx(L, 0.8, 0.14, 2.3, '#FF9EC4', k * 10.0, wy - 1.3, wz, 0.06); } });
  // 前緣柱子與腰帶
  [-1, 1].forEach(k => { Bx(L, 0.9, 10.6, 0.9, '#FF9EC4', k * 10.0, 5.3, 6.2, 0.3); });
  Bx(L, 21, 0.7, 0.9, '#FF9EC4', 0, 10.25, 6.2, 0.3);
  // 二樓地板
  const fl2 = tex(512, 256, (c, w, h) => { c.fillStyle = FLOOR2; c.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 64) for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 64) { c.fillStyle = (x + y) / 64 % 2 ? 'rgba(255,255,255,.35)' : 'rgba(187,168,255,.18)'; c.fillRect(x, y, 64, 64); } });
  Bx(L, 19.4, 0.5, 7.6, '#FFFFFF', 0, 5.0, -2.2, 0.15);
  const f2 = Pl(L, 19.2, 7.4, fl2, 0, 5.27, -2.2); f2.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; f2.receiveShadow = true;
  Bx(L, 19.4, 0.4, 0.4, '#BBA8FF', 0, 4.75, 1.6, 0.15);
  // 欄杆
  for (let i = 0; i < 17; i++) { const x = -8.8 + i * 1.1; if (x > 6.4 && x < 9) continue; Cy(L, 0.08, 0.08, 1.1, '#FFFFFF', x, 5.8, 1.45, 8); }
  Bx(L, 15.6, 0.18, 0.25, '#FFE07A', -1.3, 6.38, 1.45, 0.08);
  // 樓梯（右側）
  for (let i = 0; i < 9; i++) Bx(L, 2.4, 0.5, 0.75, ['#FFE07A', '#FFB48F', '#8EDDC0', '#8FD3FF', '#BBA8FF'][i % 5], 7.9, 0.55 + i * 0.52, 5.4 - i * 0.48, 0.12);
  // 屋頂（三角形斜屋頂＋屋簷）
  const roof = G(L, 0, 10.6, 0);
  [-1, 1].forEach(k => { const r = Bx(roof, 11.8, 0.6, 14.4, k < 0 ? '#FF8FB8' : '#FF9EC4', k * 5.15, 2.4, -0.1, 0.25); r.rotation.z = -k * 0.46; });
  const gable = new THREE.Shape(); gable.moveTo(-10, 0); gable.lineTo(10, 0); gable.lineTo(0, 4.7); gable.lineTo(-10, 0);
  const gb = mesh(new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(gable, { depth: 0.5, bevelEnabled: true, bevelThickness: 0.12, bevelSize: 0.12, bevelSegments: 3 }), MAT('#FFF4E4'), 0, -0.05, 6.0); roof.add(gb);
  const gbb = gb.clone(); gbb.position.z = -6.7; roof.add(gbb);
  // 屋頂瓦片點點
  for (let i = 0; i < 18; i++) { const k = i % 2 ? 1 : -1, j = Math.floor(i / 2); Sp(roof, 0.28, '#FFFFFF', k * (2.2 + (j % 3) * 2.8), 3.6 - (j % 3) * 1.3, -5 + (j / 3 | 0) * 3.4, 1, 0.5, 1).rotation.z = -k * 0.46; }
  // 校徽
  const emb = tex(512, 512, (c, w) => { const cx = w / 2; c.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF'; c.beginPath(); c.arc(cx, cx, 250, 0, 7); c.fill(); c.lineWidth = 22; c.strokeStyle = '#7CC56B'; c.beginPath(); c.arc(cx, cx, 226, 0, 7); c.stroke(); c.lineWidth = 6; c.strokeStyle = '#FFB2C8'; c.setLineDash([14, 12]); c.beginPath(); c.arc(cx, cx, 200, 0, 7); c.stroke(); c.setLineDash([]);
    c.fillStyle = '#E8444B'; c.beginPath(); const hx = cx, hy = 132; c.moveTo(hx, hy + 30); c.bezierCurveTo(hx - 60, hy - 10, hx - 30, hy - 55, hx, hy - 22); c.bezierCurveTo(hx + 30, hy - 55, hx + 60, hy - 10, hx, hy + 30); c.fill();
    c.fillStyle = '#5B4636'; c.textAlign = 'center'; c.textBaseline = 'middle'; c.font = `400 150px ${RF}`; c.fillText('四維', cx, 280); c.fillStyle = '#7CC56B'; c.font = `900 66px ${FONT}`; c.fillText('SWSH', cx, 400); });
  const eg = G(roof, 0, 3.15, 6.75); Cy(eg, 1.55, 1.55, 0.35, '#FFE07A', 0, 0, 0, 40).rotation.x = Math.PI / 2; const ef = mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(1.38, 40), new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ map: emb, roughness: 0.6 }), 0, 0, 0.19, false); eg.add(ef);
  ANIM.push(t => { eg.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 1.2) * 0.04; });
  // 招牌
  const signT = tex(1024, 200, (c, w, h) => { c.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF'; rr(c, 6, 6, w - 12, h - 12, 90); c.fill(); c.lineWidth = 10; c.strokeStyle = '#FFB2C8'; rr(c, 16, 16, w - 32, h - 32, 80); c.stroke(); c.fillStyle = '#5B4636'; c.textAlign = 'center'; c.textBaseline = 'middle'; c.font = `400 92px ${RF}`; c.fillText('四維圖書資訊中心', w / 2, h / 2 + 4); });
  const sg = mesh(rbox(8.4, 1.7, 0.3, 0.3), MAT('#FFE07A'), 0, 11.45, 6.95); L.add(sg); Pl(L, 8.1, 1.58, signT, 0, 11.45, 7.12);

  bench(-7.6, 18.5, 0.25); bench(-19, 9.5, 0.6);

  // 書本造型的告示牌
  const bk = G(W, -3.6, 0, 21.6); bk.rotation.y = 0.15; const bkT = tex(512, 256, (c, w, h) => { c.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF'; c.fillRect(0, 0, w, h); c.fillStyle = '#E9E2D6'; c.fillRect(w / 2 - 3, 0, 6, h); c.fillStyle = '#5B4636'; c.textAlign = 'center'; c.font = `400 42px ${RF}`; c.fillText('歡迎光臨', w / 4, 110); c.fillText('四維圖書館', w * 3 / 4, 110); c.font = `700 24px ${FONT}`; c.fillStyle = '#E0679A'; c.fillText('點點角色試試看', w / 4, 170); c.fillText('一起來讀書吧！', w * 3 / 4, 170); });

  scene.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  const bookGeo = new THREE.BoxGeometry(1, 1, 1);
  const im = new THREE.InstancedMesh(bookGeo, new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ roughness: 0.75 }), BOOKS.length); im.castShadow = true; im.receiveShadow = true;
  const dummy = new THREE.Object3D(), mtx = new THREE.Matrix4(), PAL = ['#FF8FB8', '#FFE07A', '#8EDDC0', '#8FD3FF', '#BBA8FF', '#FFB48F', '#FFFFFF', '#F7C6D9', '#A8E6CF'];
  BOOKS.forEach((b, i) => { dummy.position.set(b.x, b.y, b.z); dummy.scale.set(b.w, b.h, b.d); dummy.rotation.set(0, 0, b.rz); dummy.updateMatrix(); mtx.multiplyMatrices(b.par.matrixWorld, dummy.matrix); im.setMatrixAt(i, mtx); im.setColorAt(i, new THREE.Color(PAL[(Math.random() * PAL.length) | 0]).convertSRGBToLinear()); });
  scene.add(im);

  scene.remove(im); const isleInv = new THREE.Matrix4().copy(isle.matrixWorld).invert(); for (let i = 0; i < BOOKS.length; i++) { im.getMatrixAt(i, mtx); mtx.premultiply(isleInv); im.setMatrixAt(i, mtx); } isle.add(im);

  linearize(scene);
}

// =====================================================================
//  相機控制、點擊
// =====================================================================
function preset() {
  const p = portrait();
  camera.fov = p ? 50 : 32; camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight; camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
  Object.assign(cam, p ? { tYaw: 0.16, tPitch: 0.3, tDist: 76 } : { tYaw: 0.12, tPitch: 0.2, tDist: Math.max(56, 58 * Math.max(1, 1.55 / camera.aspect)) });
  camTarget.set(p ? 1 : 0, p ? 6.4 : 7.6, p ? 2.5 : 1);
}
function updateCam(t) {
  const k = reduceMotion ? 1 : 0.08;
  cam.yaw += (cam.tYaw - cam.yaw) * k; cam.pitch += (cam.tPitch - cam.pitch) * k; cam.dist += (cam.tDist - cam.dist) * k;
  const breathe = reduceMotion ? 0 : Math.sin(t * 0.35) * 0.02;
  const yaw = cam.yaw + breathe, cp = Math.cos(cam.pitch);
  camera.position.set(camTarget.x + Math.sin(yaw) * cp * cam.dist, camTarget.y + Math.sin(cam.pitch) * cam.dist, camTarget.z + Math.cos(yaw) * cp * cam.dist);
  camera.lookAt(camTarget);
}
const ray = new THREE.Raycaster(), ndc = new THREE.Vector2();
function pickAt(x, y) {
  ndc.set(x / innerWidth * 2 - 1, -(y / innerHeight) * 2 + 1); ray.setFromCamera(ndc, camera);
  const h = ray.intersectObjects(pickables, false)[0]; if (!h) return null;
  let o = h.object; while (o && !o.userData.hot) o = o.parent; return o ? o.userData.hot : null;
}
function setupControls() {
  const cv = renderer.domElement, ptrs = new Map(); let moved = false, down = null, pinch = 0;
  cv.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => { cv.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); ptrs.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }); if (ptrs.size === 1) { moved = false; down = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY }; } else moved = true; });
  cv.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
    if (!ptrs.has(e.pointerId)) { if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') { const id = pickAt(e.clientX, e.clientY); cv.classList.toggle('hover', !!id); hoverId = id; } return; }
    const pr = ptrs.get(e.pointerId); ptrs.set(e.pointerId, { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY });
    if (ptrs.size === 2) { const [a, b] = [...ptrs.values()], d = Math.hypot(a.x - b.x, a.y - b.y); if (pinch) cam.tDist = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(cam.tDist * pinch / d, 30, 150); pinch = d; return; }
    if (!moved && Math.hypot(e.clientX - down.x, e.clientY - down.y) > 6) moved = true;
    if (moved) { cam.tYaw = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(cam.tYaw - (e.clientX - pr.x) * 0.006, -1.1, 1.1); cam.tPitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(cam.tPitch + (e.clientY - pr.y) * 0.004, 0.08, 1.05); }
  });
  const up = e => { if (!ptrs.has(e.pointerId)) return; ptrs.delete(e.pointerId); if (ptrs.size < 2) pinch = 0; if (!ptrs.size && !moved && e.type === 'pointerup') { const id = pickAt(e.clientX, e.clientY); if (id) poke(id); } };
  cv.addEventListener('pointerup', up); cv.addEventListener('pointercancel', up);
  cv.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { hoverId = null; cv.classList.remove('hover'); });
  cv.addEventListener('wheel', e => { e.preventDefault(); cam.tDist = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(cam.tDist * (e.deltaY > 0 ? 1.1 : 0.9), 30, 150); }, { passive: false });
}
let hoverId = null;

// ---- 場景標籤 ----
const pinEls = {};
function buildPins() {
  scene.updateMatrixWorld(true); const ws = new THREE.Vector3();
  Object.entries(HOTS).forEach(([id, h]) => { h.ay *= h.g.getWorldScale(ws).y; const b = document.createElement('button'); b.type = 'button'; b.className = 'pin'; b.innerHTML = `<i style="background:${h.col}">${h.icon}</i><span class="lt">${esc(h.label)}</span>`; b.setAttribute('aria-label', h.label); b.onclick = () => poke(id); $('#pins').appendChild(b); pinEls[id] = b; });
}
const v3 = new THREE.Vector3();
function updatePins() {
  Object.entries(HOTS).forEach(([id, h]) => { h.g.getWorldPosition(v3); v3.y += h.ay; v3.project(camera); const el = pinEls[id]; if (v3.z > 1) { el.classList.add('hide'); return; } el.classList.remove('hide'); el.style.transform = `translate(${(v3.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * innerWidth}px, ${(-v3.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * innerHeight}px) translate(-50%, -100%)`; });
}
function bubble(id, text) {
  const h = HOTS[id]; if (!h) return; h.g.getWorldPosition(v3); v3.y += h.ay + 2.2; v3.project(camera);
  const b = document.createElement('div'); b.className = 'bubble'; b.textContent = text; b.style.left = (v3.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * innerWidth + 'px'; b.style.top = (-v3.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * innerHeight + 'px'; document.body.appendChild(b); setTimeout(() => b.remove(), 1300);
}

// 點到熱點：角色反應 → 開彈窗
const REACT = {
  dede: ['汪汪！', 'woof', ['dede']], xiner: ['想看什麼書呢？', 'giggle', ['xiner']], xiaosi: ['一起來自學吧！', 'tap', ['xiaosi']],
  expo: ['新書展開幕囉！', 'giggle', ['sisi', 'juanmao']], opac: ['嗶！查詢機啟動', 'tap', []], portal: ['咻～傳送中！', 'whoosh', []],
  pod: ['3、2、1，夢想啟航！', 'whoosh', []], radio: ['ON AIR 📻', 'radio', []], gacha: ['今天的運氣如何？', 'pop', []]
};
let busy = false;
function poke(id) {
  if (busy || $('#dlg').open) return; const r = REACT[id]; if (!r) return;
  busy = true; SFX[r[1]](); bubble(id, r[0]); const t = clock.getElapsedTime(); r[2].forEach(k => CHARS[k] && (CHARS[k].jump = t)); bounceHot(id, t);
  $('#hint').style.opacity = 0;
  setTimeout(() => { busy = false; openPanel(id); }, reduceMotion ? 150 : 650);
}
const bounces = {};
function bounceHot(id, t) { bounces[id] = t; }

// =====================================================================
//  彈窗內容
// =====================================================================
const dlg = $('#dlg'), sheet = $('#sheet');
dlg.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === dlg) dlg.close(); });
dlg.addEventListener('close', () => { stopAudio(); });
function frame({ hc, av, tag, title, say, body }) {
  sheet.innerHTML = `<div class="sh-head" style="--hc:${hc}"><div class="av" style="${av && av.startsWith('data:') ? `background-image:url(${av})` : ''}">${av && !av.startsWith('data:') ? av : ''}</div><div><span class="tag">${tag}</span><h2>${title}</h2></div><button class="x" type="button" aria-label="關閉">✕</button></div>${say ? `<div class="say">${say}</div>` : ''}<div class="sh-body">${body}</div>`;
  sheet.querySelector('.x').onclick = () => dlg.close();
  if (!dlg.open) { dlg.showModal(); SFX.chime(); }
  sheet.querySelector('.sh-body').scrollTop = 0;
}
function openPanel(id) { (PANELS[id] || (() => {}))(); }
const bookCover = (t, i) => `<div class="cv" style="background:${['#FFD6E5', '#FFF3C4', '#D7F5EA', '#DDF1FF', '#ECE6FF', '#FFE1D0'][i % 6]}">${['📕', '📒', '📗', '📘', '📙', '📓'][i % 6]}</div>`;
const demoTag = on => on ? '<span class="demo">示範資料</span>' : '';

function openStatus() {
  const d = new Date(), wd = d.getDay(), hm = d.getHours() * 60 + d.getMinutes(), row = CONFIG.hours.find(h => h.d.includes(wd));
  if (!row || !row.open) return { open: false, row };
  const [oh, om] = row.open.split(':').map(Number), [ch, cm] = row.close.split(':').map(Number);
  return { open: hm >= oh * 60 + om && hm < ch * 60 + cm, row };
}

const PANELS = {
  dede() {
    const st = openStatus(), wd = new Date().getDay();
    const rows = CONFIG.hours.map(h => `<tr class="${h.d.includes(wd) ? 'today' : ''}"><td>${esc(h.label)}${h.d.includes(wd) ? '（今天）' : ''}</td><td>${h.open ? `${h.open} – ${h.close}` : esc(h.note || '休館')}</td></tr>`).join('');
    const rk = CONFIG.rank.map((b, i) => `<li><span class="m">${['🥇', '🥈', '🥉'][i] || i + 1}</span><span class="t"><b>${esc(b.t)}</b><small>${esc(b.a)}</small></span></li>`).join('');
    frame({ hc: '#FFF3C4', av: IMG.dede, tag: '🐶 櫃檯值班中', title: 'DeDe 的開館小報', say: '汪！我是 DeDe～今天也趴在櫃檯等你來借書 🐾',
      body: `<div class="sec"><h3>⏰ 今日開館時間 ${demoTag(CONFIG.hoursDemo)}</h3><p><span class="status ${st.open ? '' : 'closed'}">${st.open ? '現在開館中' : '目前休館中'}</span></p><table class="hours">${rows}</table></div>
      <div class="sec"><h3>🏆 ${esc(CONFIG.rankTitle)} ${demoTag(CONFIG.rankDemo)}</h3><ol class="rank">${rk}</ol></div>
      <div class="foot">📍 ${esc(CONFIG.contact.addr)}・☎️ <a href="${CONFIG.contact.telLink}">${esc(CONFIG.contact.tel)}</a></div>` });
  },
  opac() {
    frame({ hc: '#DDF1FF', av: '🔍', tag: 'WebPAC', title: '館藏查詢機', say: '輸入書名、作者或關鍵字，按下查詢就幫你找書！',
      body: `<form class="sec" id="qf"><div class="search"><select id="qt" aria-label="查詢欄位"><option>書名</option><option>作者</option><option>關鍵字</option></select><input id="qi" placeholder="例如：小論文" aria-label="查詢關鍵字" enterkeyhint="search"><button class="btn" type="submit">查詢</button></div></form>

// ---- 廣播電台 ----
let audio = null, curEp = 0;
function stopAudio() { if (audio) { audio.pause(); audio = null; } const r = sheet.querySelector('.radio'); r && r.classList.remove('on'); }
function renderRadio(i) {
  stopAudio(); curEp = i; const E = CONFIG.radio[i];
  frame({ hc: '#FFE1D0', av: '📻', tag: '🎙️ ON AIR', title: '不可不讀廣播電台', say: '每集 10 分鐘，帶你認識一本好書。',
    body: `<div class="sec radio" id="rd"><div class="scr"><small>${esc(E.ep)}</small><b>${esc(E.t)}</b></div><div class="ctl"><button class="play" id="pl" type="button" aria-label="播放">▶</button><div class="bar" id="bar"><i></i></div><div class="eq"><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i></div></div>${E.src ? '' : '<p style="margin:8px 4px 0;font-weight:700;font-size:13px">📼 音檔準備中，敬請期待！</p>'}</div>
    <div class="sec"><h3>📋 節目列表</h3><div class="eps">${CONFIG.radio.map((e, j) => `<button type="button" class="${j === i ? 'on' : ''}" data-i="${j}"><span>${j === i ? '🔊' : '🎧'}</span><span>${esc(e.ep)}｜${esc(e.t)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></div>
    <div class="sec"><h3>✨ 本集好書金句</h3><div class="quote">「${esc(E.quote)}」<small>— ${esc(E.from)}</small></div></div>` });
  sheet.querySelectorAll('.eps button').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { SFX.pop(); renderRadio(+b.dataset.i); });
  const rd = $('#rd'), pl = $('#pl'), bar = $('#bar');
  pl.onclick = () => {
    if (!E.src) { SFX.radio(); rd.classList.add('on'); setTimeout(() => rd.classList.remove('on'), 1600); toast('這集的音檔還在錄製中，敬請期待！'); return; }
    if (audio && !audio.paused) { audio.pause(); pl.textContent = '▶'; rd.classList.remove('on'); return; }
    if (!audio) { audio = new Audio(E.src); audio.ontimeupdate = () => { bar.firstChild.style.width = (audio.currentTime / (audio.duration || 1) * 100) + '%'; }; audio.onended = () => { pl.textContent = '▶'; rd.classList.remove('on'); }; audio.onerror = () => toast('音檔載入失敗，請稍後再試'); }
    audio.play().then(() => { pl.textContent = '❚❚'; rd.classList.add('on'); }).catch(() => toast('無法播放音檔'));
  };
  bar.onclick = e => { if (audio && audio.duration) { const r = bar.getBoundingClientRect(); audio.currentTime = (e.clientX - r.left) / r.width * audio.duration; } };
}

// ---- 扭蛋：今日幸運書籤 ----
const avImgs = {}; Object.entries(IMG).forEach(([k, v]) => { if (v.startsWith('data:')) { const im = new Image(); im.src = v; avImgs[k] = im; } });
function drawBookmark(qi, dateStr) {
  const Q = QUOTES[qi], W = 600, H = 1000, c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = W; c.height = H; const x = c.getContext('2d');
  const pal = [['#FFD6E5', '#FFF3C4'], ['#DDF1FF', '#ECE6FF'], ['#D7F5EA', '#FFF3C4'], ['#FFE1D0', '#FFD6E5'], ['#ECE6FF', '#D7F5EA']][qi % 5];
  const gr = x.createLinearGradient(0, 0, 0, H); gr.addColorStop(0, pal[0]); gr.addColorStop(1, pal[1]); x.fillStyle = gr; rr(x, 0, 0, W, H, 48); x.fill();
  x.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,.75)'; rr(x, 36, 120, W - 72, 640, 36); x.fill();
  x.strokeStyle = '#FFFFFF'; x.lineWidth = 6; x.setLineDash([16, 12]); rr(x, 22, 22, W - 44, H - 44, 36); x.stroke(); x.setLineDash([]);
  x.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF'; x.beginPath(); x.arc(W / 2, 66, 22, 0, 7); x.fill(); x.fillStyle = pal[0]; x.beginPath(); x.arc(W / 2, 66, 10, 0, 7); x.fill();
  x.textAlign = 'center'; x.fillStyle = '#E0679A'; x.font = `900 26px ${FONT}`; x.fillText('四維心靈書籤', W / 2, 168);
  x.fillStyle = '#B5A595'; x.font = `700 20px ${FONT}`; x.fillText(dateStr, W / 2, 200);
  x.fillStyle = '#5B4636'; x.font = `400 44px ${RF}`; const lines = []; let line = ''; for (const ch of '「' + Q.q + '」') { if (x.measureText(line + ch).width > W - 150 && line) { lines.push(line); line = ch; } else line += ch; } lines.push(line);
  const y0 = 440 - (lines.length - 1) * 31; lines.forEach((l, i) => x.fillText(l, W / 2, y0 + i * 64));
  x.fillStyle = '#8A7260'; x.font = `700 22px ${FONT}`; x.fillText('— ' + Q.s, W / 2, y0 + lines.length * 64 + 30);
  x.fillStyle = '#FFFFFF'; rr(x, 70, 790, W - 140, 104, 52); x.fill(); x.fillStyle = '#8A7260'; x.font = `700 20px ${FONT}`; x.fillText('📚 推薦你到圖書館借', W / 2, 830); x.fillStyle = '#5B4636'; x.font = `400 32px ${RF}`; x.fillText('《' + Q.b + '》', W / 2, 873);
  const ks = ['xiner', 'xiaosi', 'sisi', 'juanmao', 'dede'], k = ks[qi % ks.length], im = avImgs[k];
  if (im && im.complete && im.naturalWidth) { x.save(); x.beginPath(); x.arc(W / 2, 940, 34, 0, 7); x.clip(); x.drawImage(im, W / 2 - 34, 906, 68, 68); x.restore(); }
  x.fillStyle = '#B5A595'; x.font = `700 16px ${FONT}`; x.fillText('SWSH LIBRARY・花蓮四維高中圖資中心', W / 2, H - 10 > 985 ? 985 : H - 10);
  return c.toDataURL('image/png');
}
async function gachaPanel() {
  const st = store.get(), today = todayStr(), done = st.gacha && st.gacha.date === today;
  const d = new Date(), ds = `${d.getFullYear()}.${String(d.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}.${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const show = (qi, fresh) => {
    const url = drawBookmark(qi, ds), Q = QUOTES[qi];
    frame({ hc: '#FFF3C4', av: '🎁', tag: fresh ? '✨ 恭喜轉到！' : '🎁 今天已經轉過囉', title: '今日幸運書籤', say: fresh ? '這是今天專屬於你的心靈書籤，長按圖片就能存起來分享！' : '每天只能轉一次喔！這是你今天的書籤，明天再來試試手氣 🍀',
      body: `<div class="sec mark"><img src="${url}" alt="四維心靈書籤：${esc(Q.q)}"><p>📱 手機：長按圖片 → 儲存圖片／分享<br>💻 電腦：點下方按鈕下載</p><div class="row" style="justify-content:center"><a class="btn" href="${url}" download="四維心靈書籤-${ds}.png">⬇️ 下載書籤</a><button class="btn alt" type="button" id="share" ${navigator.share ? '' : 'hidden'}>📤 分享</button></div></div>` });
    const sh = $('#share'); if (sh) sh.onclick = async () => { try { const blob = await (await fetch(url)).blob(), f = new File([blob], 'swsh-bookmark.png', { type: 'image/png' }); if (navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [f] })) await navigator.share({ files: [f], title: '四維心靈書籤', text: Q.q }); else await navigator.share({ title: '四維心靈書籤', text: `「${Q.q}」— ${Q.s}` }); } catch (e) {} };
  };
  if (done) { show(st.gacha.idx, false); return; }
  frame({ hc: '#FFF3C4', av: '🎁', tag: '🎰 每天一次', title: '今日幸運書籤扭蛋', say: '轉轉看！掉出來的扭蛋裡，有一句送給你的話和一本推薦書 💫',
    body: `<div class="sec" style="text-align:center"><div style="font-size:84px;line-height:1.1" id="gEmoji">🎁</div><p style="font-weight:700;color:var(--ink2)">每人每天可以轉一次，轉到的書籤會保留到今天結束。</p><button class="btn" type="button" id="spin" style="font-size:18px;padding:14px 30px">🎲 轉扭蛋！</button></div>` });
  $('#spin').onclick = async () => {
    $('#spin').disabled = true; $('#gEmoji').textContent = '🌀';
    const qi = Math.floor(Math.random() * QUOTES.length); store.set({ gacha: { date: today, idx: qi } });
    dlg.close(); await spinGacha(); SFX.sparkle(); show(qi, true);
  };
}
function spinGacha() {
  return new Promise(res => {
    const G0 = CHARS.gacha; SFX.crank(); const t0 = clock.getElapsedTime();
    G0.outCap.visible = false;
    gachaAnim = t => { const u = (t - t0) / (reduceMotion ? 0.2 : 1.6);
      if (u < 0.55) { G0.crank.rotation.z = -u / 0.55 * Math.PI * 4; G0.caps.forEach((c, i) => { c.rotation.x += 0.08; c.position.y += Math.sin(t * 30 + i) * 0.01; }); }
      else if (u < 1) { if (!G0.outCap.visible) { G0.outCap.visible = true; SFX.drop(); } const v = (u - 0.55) / 0.45; G0.outCap.position.set(0, 0.55 + Math.abs(Math.sin(v * Math.PI * 2)) * 0.8 * (1 - v), 1.4 + v * 1.4); G0.outCap.rotation.x = v * 8; }
      else { gachaAnim = null; G0.outCap.rotation.x = 0; setTimeout(() => { G0.outCap.visible = false; res(); }, reduceMotion ? 0 : 250); } };
  });
}
let gachaAnim = null;

// =====================================================================
//  主迴圈
// =====================================================================
const clock = new THREE.Clock();
function loop() {
  requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  const t = clock.getElapsedTime();
  if (!reduceMotion) ANIM.forEach(f => f(t));
  // 角色呼吸＋跳躍
  Object.values(CHARS).forEach(c => { if (!c.body) return; const j = t - c.jump; const jy = j < 0.6 ? Math.sin(j / 0.6 * Math.PI) * 0.9 : 0;
    if (!reduceMotion) { c.body.scale.y = 1 + Math.sin(t * 2.2 + c.ph) * 0.018; if (c.head) c.head.rotation.z = Math.sin(t * 1.1 + c.ph) * 0.06; }
    c.body.position.y = (c.baseY ?? (c.baseY = c.body.position.y)) + jy;
    if (c.tail) c.tail.rotation.y = Math.sin(t * (j < 1.5 ? 18 : 6)) * 0.6; });
  // 熱點彈跳＋滑鼠懸停
  Object.entries(HOTS).forEach(([id, h]) => { const b = t - (bounces[id] ?? -9); const s0 = h.g.userData.s0 ?? (h.g.userData.s0 = h.g.scale.x); let s = s0 * (hoverId === id ? 1.04 : 1); if (b < 0.5) s *= 1 + Math.sin(b / 0.5 * Math.PI) * 0.08; h.g.scale.setScalar(h.g.scale.x + (s - h.g.scale.x) * 0.3); });
  if (gachaAnim) gachaAnim(t);
  updateCam(t); renderer.render(scene, camera); updatePins();
}

const bs = $('#bSound'); const syncSound = () => { bs.classList.toggle('off', !SFX.on); bs.firstChild.textContent = SFX.on ? '🔊' : '🔇'; }; syncSound();
bs.onclick = () => { SFX.toggle(); syncSound(); };
$('#bView').onclick = () => { SFX.pop(); preset(); };
addEventListener('resize', () => { if (!renderer) return; renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight); camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight; preset(); });
function start() {
  if (!renderer) { $('#loading').remove(); $('#stage').innerHTML = '<div class="nogl">😢 你的瀏覽器不支援 3D 顯示<br>請用下方按鈕使用圖書館的各項功能！</div>'; return; }
  buildScene(); buildPins(); setupControls(); preset(); cam.yaw = cam.tYaw + 0.5; cam.pitch = cam.tPitch + 0.25; cam.dist = cam.tDist * 1.35; requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  setTimeout(() => { const l = $('#loading'); l.style.opacity = 0; setTimeout(() => l.remove(), 700); }, 400);
  setTimeout(() => { $('#hint').style.opacity = 0; }, 9000);
}
(document.fonts && document.fonts.ready ? Promise.race([document.fonts.ready, new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2500))]) : Promise.resolve()).then(start);
window.__lib = { scene, cam, camera, poke, HOTS, CHARS, target: camTarget };
</script>
</body>
</html>
